  %t766 = getelementptr inbounds { ptr, i64, i64 }, ptr %t765, i32 0, i32 0

  %t768 = load ptr, ptr %t766
  %t769 = load i64, ptr %t767

  br label %deep.rel.arr.cond.99
deep.rel.arr.cond.99:
  %t771 = load i64, ptr %t770
  %t772 = icmp ult i64 %t771, %t769
  br i1 %t772, label %deep.rel.arr.body.99, label %deep.rel.arr.done.99
deep.rel.arr.body.99:

deep.rel.arr.done.99:

  store %"struct.std/io.Poller" zeroinitializer, ptr %t744

  %t776 = load i32, ptr %t775
  %t777 = icmp eq i32 %t776, 0
  br i1 %t777, label %deep.rel.adt.100.0, label %deep.rel.adt.done.100.0
deep.rel.adt.100.0:
  %t778 = getelementptr inbounds { i32, [4 x i8], %interface.Error }, ptr %t775, i32 0, i32 2
  store i64 zeroinitializer, ptr %t778
  br label %deep.rel.adt.done.100.0
deep.rel.adt.done.100.0:
  %t779 = icmp eq i32 %t776, 1
  br i1 %t779, label %deep.rel.adt.101.1, label %deep.rel.adt.done.101.1
deep.rel.adt.101.1:
  %t780 = getelementptr inbounds { i32, [4 x i8], %interface.Error }, ptr %t775, i32 0, i32 2
  %t781 = getelementptr inbounds %interface.Error, ptr %t780, i32 0, i32 0
  %t782 = getelementptr inbounds %interface.Error, ptr %t780, i32 0, i32 1
  %t783 = load ptr, ptr %t781
  %t784 = load ptr, ptr %t782
  %t785 = icmp eq ptr %t783, null
  br i1 %t785, label %iface.rel.empty.102, label %iface.rel.have.102
iface.rel.empty.102:
  br label %iface.rel.done.102
iface.rel.have.102:
  %t786 = getelementptr inbounds [6 x ptr], ptr %t784, i64 0, i64 3

  call void %t787(ptr %t783)

  store ptr null, ptr %t781
  store ptr null, ptr %t782
  br label %iface.rel.done.102
iface.rel.done.102:
  store %interface.Error zeroinitializer, ptr %t780
  br label %deep.rel.adt.done.101.1
deep.rel.adt.done.101.1:
  store { i32, [4 x i8], %interface.Error } zeroinitializer, ptr %t775
  ret void
}

define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()

  %t791 = icmp ne i32 %t789, 0
  br i1 %t791, label %main.async.fail.103, label %main.async.ok.103
main.async.fail.103:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.103:
  %t792 = load i32, ptr %t790
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t792
}
